# Why It Sucks to Want Clear Skin (in Elizabethan England) — Image Prompts

311 beats · ~3–5 s each · Visual Style Profile locked · Characters locked to the Yesteryears character sheets

**Headwear rule:** headwear fits the character's class and the scene. Nobody wears a hat indoors or in any bad scenario. As a girl outdoors, you wear the plain linen bonnet your mother ties each morning (the script calls for it); as an adult, you are always bareheaded. The only other caps are the two the script names: Joan pulling her cap lower, and you taking yours off before stepping into the sun.

**Character lock**

| Story role | Sheet |
|---|---|
| You, age 5 / 8 | Girl variant, with freckles |
| You, 16 / 22 / 24 / 40 | Adult woman variant, auburn-haired (natural, painted and unpainted states) |
| Your mother | Adult woman variant |

| The apothecary | Old man variant |
| Father, the Countess, Joan, steward, physician, Queen Elizabeth | Not on a sheet (fixed descriptions) |
